# [ckpt, data, docs] refactor: restructure tools/ into self-contained atomic tool dirs

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/baidu-baige/LoongForge/pull/263) · state: closed · merged: True

## Original PR description

  ## Summary
  - Reorganize `tools/` into self-contained, clearly-named "atomic tool" directories so each
    tool is discoverable and runnable on its own, with a top-level `tools/README.md` index.
  - Align all offline-packing / checkpoint / data-preprocess docs and test harnesses with the
    new paths and the WDS-native `wds_pack.cli` workflow.
  - Deprecate the redundant `--max-packed-tokens` training arg in favor of `--seq-length`
    (no change for any existing config; see the note under Train), and fix a latent circular
    import surfaced during the move.

  ## Changes

  ### Tool directory restructure (renames, no logic change)
  - `tools/convert_checkpoint`                      → `tools/mcore_checkpoint_convert`
  - `tools/dist_checkpoint`                         → `tools/mcore_bridge`
  - `tools/data_preprocess/vlm`                     → `tools/vlm_data_preprocess`
  - `tools/data_preprocess/llm`                     → `tools/llm_data_preprocess`
  - `tools/data_preprocess/embodied/dreamzero`      → `tools/dreamzero_data_preprocess`
  - `loongforge/embodied/tools/dcp_to_safetensors.py` → `tools/torch_dcp_convert/`
  - `tools/benchmark_te_parallel_layers.py`         → `tools/te_parallel_benchmark/` (+README)
  - `loongforge/embodied/tools/ego2robot`           → `tools/ego2robot` (PROJECT_ROOT→TOOL_ROOT)
  - Internal imports updated to match the new package names; dead `sys.path` hacks removed.
  - Added usage READMEs for each promoted tool + a top-level `tools/README.md` capability index.

  ### Train
  - Deprecate `--max-packed-tokens` (default `8192`→`None`). The validator always resolves it
    to `--seq-length` (whenever `seq_length` is set) and warns when an explicitly passed value
    differs. Every active config already passes a value equal to its `seq_length`, so existing
    configs behave identically.
    **Note:** a user-supplied value that differs from `seq_length` is now overridden (with a
    warning) instead of being used.
  - Fix a pre-existing circular import in `loongforge/utils/global_vars.py` (TYPE_CHECKING +
    lazy runtime import of ChatTemplate), surfaced when `loongforge.data` is imported first.

  ### Tests
  - Move the mcore_bridge roundtrip suite to `tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/` and add
    `tests/__init__.py` so the test package no longer shadows `tools/mcore_bridge` under
    `pytest tests/...`.
  - Add unit tests for `BaseTaskEncoder.pack_selected_samples`.
  - Point the `offline_packing` test harness at the new tool path and the WDS-native
    `wds_pack.cli` steps (the CI case stays disabled, see "Not verified" below).

  ### Docs
  - Rewrite data-preprocess / offline-packing / mcore_bridge docs (EN + ZH) to the new paths
    and the 4-step `wds_pack.cli` pipeline.

  ## Test Plan
  - `pytest tests/ --collect-only`: 158 collected / 9 errors, all 9 are pre-existing env
    issues (HyperHead / torch-FSDP), none from the rename.
  - CI whitelist + runnable units: 56 passed, 1 skipped (includes `test_task_encoder_packing`,
    `test_kimi_k3` and `test_convert_checkpoint_config_utils` on the renamed packages).
  - Offline packing: full end-to-end run on mock WDS (24 text samples) through
    scan → pack_bins (BFD) → build_plan → write_wds; all pack invariants hold, energon metadata
    written, guardrails (bad sample_type / chat_mix-without-template) behave correctly.
  - All 276 changed `.sh` pass `bash -n`; all 143 changed `.py` parse; all changed YAML parse;
    every `tools/...` path referenced in tracked files resolves; a stale old-path sweep across
    tracked files is clean.
  - A trial merge against current upstream master (c0aee8e) is conflict-free and introduces no
    references to the old paths.
  - `max_packed_tokens` validator exercised across all branches; no new warnings fire for
    existing configs.

  ## Not verified / known limitations
  - The `offline_packing` step in `tests/llm_vlm/configs/qwen2.5_vl_7b.yaml` stays
    `RUNNABLE_FLAG: false`. The WDS-native flow was only run end-to-end on mock text WDS, not
    on real image/video data.
  - mcore_bridge roundtrip numerics were not run (they need the project Docker image).
  - Import convention: self-references are bare (`mcore_bridge.*`, `mcore_checkpoint_convert.*`)
    and cross-package references are `tools.`-qualified. Unifying everything to bare imports and
    dropping `tools/__init__.py` is intentionally deferred to a follow-up.

## Verdict: Approve — can be merged

The move is clean and I found no regressions; the one end-to-end check that was missing has been added. **One 1-line change is recommended before merge** (issue 1); the rest are FYI.

## What I tested

**Static checks — all green**

- **Move integrity**: compared all 7 moved directory groups file-by-file between base and head — **no files lost**; every content difference is explained by an old-path → new-path substitution.
- **Stale-path sweep**: `tools/convert_checkpoint`, `tools/dist_checkpoint`, `tools/data_preprocess`, `tools/dcp_to_safetensors.py`, `loongforge/embodied/tools` (including dotted forms) — **0 hits**. A reference-existence check over 660 `tools/…` / `tests/…` references found no newly-broken paths and **4 previously-stale paths fixed**.
- `bash -n` on every tracked `.sh` (555), YAML parse (298), `ruff check` on the changed `.py` (143), `ci/check_doc_links.py --changed-since <base>`, and SPDX headers on the 18 added files — **all pass**.

**Dynamic tests — with Loong-Megatron cloned (pinned commit) and 8×A800**

- `pytest tests/ --collect-only`: PR **233** / base **220**, **0 errors**.
- **Full A/B run**: PR `23 failed / 166 passed / 2 skipped / 4 errors` vs base `23 / 158 / 1 / 0` — the **failure sets are identical test-by-test** (`diff` empty), i.e. **no regressions**.
- New unit test `test_task_encoder_packing.py`: **8 passed, 1 skipped**.
- **Circular-import fix**: base reproduces `ImportError: cannot import name 'ChatTemplate' from partially initialized module 'loongforge.data'`; the PR is clean.
- **`--max-packed-tokens` validator**: exercised directly with 6 cases — behaves exactly as designed.
- **End-to-end roundtrip** (`tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/qwen2.5/0.5b_bridge_roundtrip.sh`, 4 GPUs TP2×PP2, Qwen2.5-0.5B): **290/290 tensors bit-identical, max_abs_diff = 0.0, exit 0**. This is the most important one — it proves the moved `hf_roundtrip_test.py` and the renamed `mcore_bridge` work end-to-end, not merely that they import.
- **HF online training, end-to-end** — this exercises the exact code path the PR rewired (`training_utils.py` → `mcore_bridge`). A Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct HF checkpoint was trained **directly from the HF directory** with `--load <HF dir>` (online load, no offline conversion), 1 GPU, 2 iters, seq 512, micro-batch 1:
  - `HF checkpoint loaded and sharded successfully!`
  - `lm loss: 4.0974 → 4.0679`, grad norm `79.14 → 80.22` — the loss actually goes down
  - `--save-hf true` wrote a valid HF checkpoint back (290 tensors, 988 MB, standard `qwen2` config)
  - the saved weights differ from the source checkpoint (`model.layers.0.self_attn.q_proj.weight`, `max|diff| = 1.53e-05`), i.e. training really updated them
  - exit 0
  - This complements the roundtrip test above, which runs with `--train-iters 0` and therefore never touches the training loop.
  - *(Run on this PR + PR #265's fix, since without it `import loongforge` fails on the pinned transformers — see the note at the end. The training path itself is untouched by either PR.)*

## Remaining issues

**1. (Recommend fixing in this PR — 1 line) Four pseudo-tests are collected by pytest**
In `tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/test_hf_checkpoint_converter.py`, the four `test_*` functions are actually helpers: they take positional args (`tp/pp/vpp/...`) and are driven by the `__main__` block, whose real entry point is `test_hf_checkpoint_converter.sh`. After the move into `tests/`, pytest collects them as four test cases that always fail with `E fixture 'tp' not found`. In the A/B run this shows up as 4 errors on the PR vs 0 on base — exactly these four.

**They must not be deleted** (the `__main__` block calls them in 13 places). One line fixes it:

```python
# This module is a script, not a pytest suite: the test_* helpers below are
# driven by the __main__ block, which test_hf_checkpoint_converter.sh invokes.
__test__ = False
```

Verified: `pytest tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/ --collect-only` → `no tests collected`, and running the file as a script behaves identically to base.

**2. (Medium — heads-up for downstream) Silent semantic change of `--max-packed-tokens`**
The flag now defaults to `--seq-length` instead of `8192`. I checked all 41 references in the repo: **every current config is safe** (all VLM configs with packing enabled pass a value equal to `seq_length`; LLM families early-return from the validator and their code paths never read the field). But for any **future** "VLM family + packing enabled + flag not passed", the packed length silently changes from 8192 to `seq_length` — a warning is only emitted when an *explicit different* value is passed, never when the flag is omitted. Worth a line in the migration notes.

**3. (Medium — pre-existing, but this PR rewrote exactly these lines) PYTHONPATH order in the roundtrip script**
`test_hf_checkpoint_converter.sh` sets `PYTHONPATH=$MEGATRON_PATH:$LOONGFORGE_PATH:$LOONGFORGE_PATH/tools`. `/workspace/Loong-Megatron/tools` is a real package, so `import tools` resolves to Megatron's `tools` and the script dies with:

```
ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'tools.mcore_checkpoint_convert'
```

Putting the repo root first fixes it. Base fails the same way (`tools.dist_checkpoint`), so this is **not introduced here** — but since the PR rewrote these imports, it would be good to fix the order in the same pass.

**4. (Low) Scope creep**: the HelloGitHub badge, the `.gitignore` embodied-atlas entry, and `<details>` → `<details open>` are unrelated to the tools restructure. Please split them out or at least call them out in the description.

**5. (Low, pre-existing)**: `tests/llm_vlm/unit/test_kimi_k3.py` fails standalone with `ModuleNotFoundError` yet passes under a full `pytest tests/` run — it only works by accident of import order. And `tools/te_parallel_benchmark/benchmark_te_parallel_layers.py` imports `tests.unit_tests`, which does not exist. Neither is introduced by this PR.

---

## Note: an unrelated issue found while testing — already fixed by PR #265

While setting up the environment I found that **no `transformers` 5.x version allows `import loongforge`** on this revision:

- `transformers < 5.6` → `loongforge/models/common/base_model_config.py:48`
  `AttributeError: type object 'PreTrainedConfig' has no attribute '__post_init__'`
- `transformers >= 5.6` → `loongforge/models/foundation/glm5_next/glm5_next_config.py:52`
  `TypeError: non-default argument 'vision_config' follows default argument`

Root cause of the latter: transformers' `PreTrainedConfig.__init_subclass__` already applies `@dataclass` to every subclass, and LoongForge applies its own `@dataclass` on top. CPython **deletes the class attribute** of a field declared with `field(default_factory=...)` during the first pass, so the second pass sees it as an annotated-but-required field. Because `loongforge/models/__init__.py` imports `glm5_next` unconditionally, **that one class makes every model unimportable** — and the `gpu` extra pins `transformers==5.12.1`, so the documented install produces an unusable environment. Introduced by `f4e3dc9` (PR #230).

**This is fixed by PR #265 (`27064f0`).** I verified it with `transformers 5.12.1` and **no workarounds**:

- `import loongforge` OK, `Glm5NextConfig()` constructs (`vision_config.depth = 24`)
- Full scan of every `loongforge.models` submodule: **0 offending classes** (was 1)
- `pytest tests/ --collect-only`: **220 collected / 0 errors**
- **This PR + #265 combined** running the roundtrip: still **290/290 bit-identical, exit 0**

The two PRs share the same base (`359b894`) and touch disjoint files, so they are **independent, conflict-free, and can be merged in either order**.

**Problem**: `tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/test_hf_checkpoint_converter.sh:83` puts `$MEGATRON_PATH` before `$LOONGFORGE_PATH`, and since `/workspace/Loong-Megatron/tools` is a real package, `import tools` resolves to Megatron's copy instead of ours — so the script dies at import time with `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'tools.mcore_checkpoint_convert'`.

**Fix**: put `$LOONGFORGE_PATH` first (one line).

```diff
-PYTHONPATH=$MEGATRON_PATH:$LOONGFORGE_PATH:$LOONGFORGE_PATH/tools:$PYTHONPATH \
+PYTHONPATH=$LOONGFORGE_PATH:$LOONGFORGE_PATH/tools:$MEGATRON_PATH:$PYTHONPATH \
     python $LOONGFORGE_PATH/tests/mcore_bridge_roundtrip/test_hf_checkpoint_converter.py
```
